# GEMINI_API_KEY: Required for Gemini AI API calls.
# AI Studio automatically injects this at runtime from user secrets.
# Users configure this via the Secrets panel in the AI Studio UI.
GEMINI_API_KEY="MY_GEMINI_API_KEY"

# APP_URL: The URL where this applet is hosted.
# AI Studio automatically injects this at runtime with the Cloud Run service URL.
# Used for self-referential links, OAuth callbacks, and API endpoints.
APP_URL="MY_APP_URL"

# Run and deploy your AI Studio app

This contains everything you need to run your app locally.

View your app in AI Studio: https://ai.studio/apps/bb269e01-e0dd-4bda-8b78-94b054056266

## Run Locally

**Prerequisites:**  Node.js


1. Install dependencies:
   `npm install`
2. Set the `GEMINI_API_KEY` in [.env.local](.env.local) to your Gemini API key
3. Run the app:
   `npm run dev`

FILE: src/App.tsx
------
/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Header } from './components/Header';
import { Sidebar } from './components/Sidebar';
import { GraphWorkspace } from './components/GraphWorkspace';
import { PropertiesPanel } from './components/PropertiesPanel';
import { TemplateLibrary } from './components/TemplateLibrary';
import { SpreadsheetView } from './components/SpreadsheetView';
import { useStore } from './store/useStore';
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';

export default function App() {
  const leftPanelOpen = useStore((state) => state.leftPanelOpen);
  const rightPanelOpen = useStore((state) => state.rightPanelOpen);
  const toggleLeftPanel = useStore((state) => state.toggleLeftPanel);
  const toggleRightPanel = useStore((state) => state.toggleRightPanel);
  const [showTemplates, setShowTemplates] = useState(false);
  const [showSpreadsheet, setShowSpreadsheet] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-screen w-full bg-white text-slate-900 overflow-hidden">
      <Header 
        onOpenTemplates={() => setShowTemplates(true)} 
        onToggleSpreadsheet={() => setShowSpreadsheet(!showSpreadsheet)}
        showSpreadsheet={showSpreadsheet}
      />
      <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden relative">
        <div className={`transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out ${leftPanelOpen ? 'w-80' : 'w-0'} overflow-hidden shrink-0 border-r border-slate-200 bg-white z-10`}>
          <div className="w-80 h-full">
            <Sidebar />
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col relative bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden">
          <button 
            onClick={toggleLeftPanel}
            className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-30 p-1 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-r-md shadow-sm hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-500"
            title={leftPanelOpen ? "Collapse Sidebar" : "Expand Sidebar"}
          >
            {leftPanelOpen ? <ChevronLeft size={16} /> : <ChevronRight size={16} />}
          </button>
          
          <button 
            onClick={toggleRightPanel}
            className="absolute right-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-30 p-1 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-l-md shadow-sm hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-500"
            title={rightPanelOpen ? "Collapse Properties" : "Expand Properties"}
          >
            {rightPanelOpen ? <ChevronRight size={16} /> : <ChevronLeft size={16} />}
          </button>

          <div className="flex-1 relative">
            <GraphWorkspace />
          </div>
          {showSpreadsheet && (
            <div className="h-1/3 min-h-[250px] border-t border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-[0_-4px_6px_-1px_rgba(0,0,0,0.05)] z-20">
              <SpreadsheetView />
            </div>
          )}
        </main>

        <div className={`transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out ${rightPanelOpen ? 'w-80' : 'w-0'} overflow-hidden shrink-0 border-l border-slate-200 bg-white z-10`}>
          <div className="w-80 h-full">
            <PropertiesPanel />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      {showTemplates && <TemplateLibrary onClose={() => setShowTemplates(false)} />}
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/GraphWorkspace.tsx
------
import React, { useCallback, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  ReactFlow,
  MiniMap,
  Controls,
  Background,
  useNodesState,
  useEdgesState,
  addEdge,
  Connection,
  Edge,
  Node,
  NodeTypes,
  Handle,
  Position,
  BackgroundVariant,
  Panel,
  useReactFlow,
  ReactFlowProvider
} from '@xyflow/react';
import '@xyflow/react/dist/style.css';
import { useStore, getVisibleNodes } from '../store/useStore';
import { BaseNode, NodeType } from '../types';
import { Layers, LayoutTemplate, Box, Component, ToggleLeft, RefreshCcw, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Focus, ChevronRight as ChevronRightIcon } from 'lucide-react';

const typeIcons: Record<NodeType, React.ReactNode> = {
  System: <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />,
  Zone: <LayoutTemplate className="w-4 h-4" />,
  Module: <Box className="w-4 h-4" />,
  Container: <Component className="w-4 h-4" />,
  Control: <ToggleLeft className="w-4 h-4" />,
};

const typeColors: Record<NodeType, string> = {
  System: 'bg-white border-slate-800 text-slate-900 shadow-md',
  Zone: 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-400 text-slate-800',
  Module: 'bg-white border-slate-300 text-slate-800',
  Container: 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700',
  Control: 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-700 shadow-sm',
};

const CustomNode = ({ data, selected }: any) => {
  const node = data.node as BaseNode;
  const isSelected = selected || data.isSelected;
  const isCollapsed = data.isCollapsed;
  const isFocused = data.isFocused;
  const isStrictAncestor = data.isStrictAncestor;
  const hasChildren = node.children.length > 0;
  const toggleCollapse = data.toggleCollapse;
  const setFocus = data.setFocus;

  // Calculate stats for collapsed state
  const getStats = () => {
    if (!hasChildren) return null;
    let childCount = 0;
    let unresolvedCount = 0;
    let conflictCount = 0;

    const countRecursive = (id: string) => {
      const n = data.allNodes[id];
      if (!n) return;
      childCount++;
      if (n.metadata?.unresolved_fields?.length) unresolvedCount++;
      if (n.mergeMetadata) {
        Object.values(n.mergeMetadata).forEach((m: any) => {
          if (m.merge_state === 'CONFLICT') conflictCount++;
        });
      }
      n.children.forEach(countRecursive);
    };

    node.children.forEach(countRecursive);

    return { childCount, unresolvedCount, conflictCount };
  };

  const stats = isCollapsed && !isStrictAncestor ? getStats() : null;

  return (
    <div
      className={`px-5 py-4 rounded-xl border-2 min-w-[240px] transition-all ${
        typeColors[node.nodeType]
      } ${isSelected ? 'ring-4 ring-emerald-500/30 border-emerald-500' : ''} ${isStrictAncestor ? 'opacity-70' : ''}`}
    >
      <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} className="w-3 h-3 !bg-slate-400 border-2 border-white" />
      
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {typeIcons[node.nodeType]}
          <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest opacity-60">{node.nodeType}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          {hasChildren && !isStrictAncestor && (
            <button
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setFocus(isFocused ? null : node.id); }}
              className={`p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-200/50 transition-colors ${isFocused ? 'text-blue-600 bg-blue-50' : 'text-slate-400'}`}
              title={isFocused ? "Clear Focus" : "Focus Subtree"}
            >
              <Focus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
          {hasChildren && !isStrictAncestor && (
            <button
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleCollapse(node.id); }}
              className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-200/50 transition-colors"
              title={isCollapsed ? "Expand Branch" : "Collapse Branch"}
            >
              {isCollapsed ? <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight break-words">{node.label}</div>
      
      {node.metadata?.confidence !== undefined && (!isCollapsed || isStrictAncestor) && (
        <div className="text-[11px] mt-3 font-medium text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
          Confidence: {Math.round(node.metadata.confidence * 100)}%
        </div>
      )}

      {isCollapsed && !isStrictAncestor && stats && (
        <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200/50 flex gap-3 text-xs font-medium">
          <span className="text-slate-500">{stats.childCount} children</span>
          {stats.unresolvedCount > 0 && <span className="text-amber-600">{stats.unresolvedCount} unresolved</span>}
          {stats.conflictCount > 0 && <span className="text-red-600">{stats.conflictCount} conflicts</span>}
        </div>
      )}

      <Handle type="source" position={Position.Bottom} className="w-3 h-3 !bg-slate-400 border-2 border-white" />
    </div>
  );
};

const nodeTypes: NodeTypes = {
  custom: CustomNode,
};

function Breadcrumb() {
  const { document, focusNodeId, setFocusNode } = useStore();
  
  if (!document || !focusNodeId) return null;

  const path: BaseNode[] = [];
  let curr = focusNodeId;
  while (curr && document.nodes[curr]) {
    path.unshift(document.nodes[curr]);
    curr = document.nodes[curr].parentId || '';
  }

  return (
    <Panel position="top-left" className="!m-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-slate-200 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
        <button 
          onClick={() => setFocusNode(null)}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors"
        >
          <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />
          Full Graph
        </button>
        {path.map((node, i) => (
          <div key={node.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ChevronRightIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
            <button
              onClick={() => setFocusNode(node.id)}
              className={`hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors ${i === path.length - 1 ? 'text-slate-900 font-bold' : ''}`}
            >
              {node.label}
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </Panel>
  );
}

function GraphView() {
  const { document, selectNode, updateNodePosition, autoLayout, collapsedNodes, focusNodeId, toggleNodeCollapse, setFocusNode } = useStore();
  const { fitView } = useReactFlow();

  // Auto-fit view when focus changes
  useEffect(() => {
    setTimeout(() => {
      fitView({ duration: 800, padding: 0.2 });
    }, 50);
  }, [focusNodeId, fitView]);

  const visibleNodesSet = useMemo(() => {
    if (!document) return new Set<string>();
    return getVisibleNodes(document.nodes, collapsedNodes, focusNodeId);
  }, [document, collapsedNodes, focusNodeId]);

  const nodes: Node[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (!document) return [];

    const strictAncestors = new Set<string>();
    if (focusNodeId && document.nodes[focusNodeId]) {
      let curr = document.nodes[focusNodeId].parentId;
      while (curr) {
        strictAncestors.add(curr);
        curr = document.nodes[curr].parentId;
      }
    }

    return Object.values(document.nodes)
      .filter(node => visibleNodesSet.has(node.id))
      .map((node) => {
        const pos = document.layout.nodePositions[node.id] || { x: 0, y: 0 };
        return {
          id: node.id,
          type: 'custom',
          position: pos,
          data: { 
            node, 
            isSelected: document.layout.selectedNode === node.id,
            isCollapsed: !!collapsedNodes[node.id],
            isFocused: focusNodeId === node.id,
            isStrictAncestor: strictAncestors.has(node.id),
            allNodes: document.nodes,
            toggleCollapse: toggleNodeCollapse,
            setFocus: setFocusNode
          },
        };
      });
  }, [document, visibleNodesSet, collapsedNodes, focusNodeId, toggleNodeCollapse, setFocusNode]);

  const edges: Edge[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (!document) return [];
    const newEdges: Edge[] = [];
    Object.values(document.nodes).forEach((node) => {
      if (!visibleNodesSet.has(node.id)) return;
      node.children.forEach((childId) => {
        if (!visibleNodesSet.has(childId)) return;
        newEdges.push({
          id: `e-${node.id}-${childId}`,
          source: node.id,
          target: childId,
          type: 'smoothstep',
          animated: false,
          style: { stroke: '#cbd5e1', strokeWidth: 2 },
        });
      });
    });
    return newEdges;
  }, [document, visibleNodesSet]);

  const onNodesChange = useCallback(
    (changes: any[]) => {
      changes.forEach((change) => {
        if (change.type === 'position' && change.position) {
          updateNodePosition(change.id, change.position);
        }
      });
    },
    [updateNodePosition]
  );

  const onNodeClick = useCallback(
    (_: React.MouseEvent, node: Node) => {
      selectNode(node.id);
    },
    [selectNode]
  );

  const onPaneClick = useCallback(() => {
    selectNode(null);
  }, [selectNode]);

  const onNodesDelete = useCallback(
    (nodesToDelete: Node[]) => {
      nodesToDelete.forEach((node) => {
        useStore.getState().deleteNode(node.id);
      });
    },
    []
  );

  if (!document) {
    return (
      <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-50/50">
        <div className="text-center text-slate-500 max-w-md p-8 bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-sm rounded-xl">
          <Layers className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-4 text-slate-300" />
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-2">No Hierarchy Loaded</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Use the sidebar to ingest a UI description or screenshot, or manually add a root node to start building.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ReactFlow
      nodes={nodes}
      edges={edges}
      onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
      onNodesDelete={onNodesDelete}
      onNodeClick={onNodeClick}
      onPaneClick={onPaneClick}
      nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
      fitView
      minZoom={0.1}
      maxZoom={4}
    >
      <Background variant={BackgroundVariant.Dots} gap={16} size={1} color="#e2e8f0" />
      <Controls />
      <Breadcrumb />
      <MiniMap 
        nodeColor={(n) => {
          const node = n.data?.node as BaseNode;
          if (!node) return '#e2e8f0';
          switch (node.nodeType) {
            case 'System': return '#334155';
            case 'Zone': return '#94a3b8';
            case 'Module': return '#cbd5e1';
            case 'Container': return '#e2e8f0';
            case 'Control': return '#f1f5f9';
            default: return '#e2e8f0';
          }
        }}
        maskColor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8)"
      />
      <Panel position="top-right">
        <button
          onClick={autoLayout}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-sm rounded-md text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors"
        >
          <RefreshCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
          Auto Layout
        </button>
      </Panel>
    </ReactFlow>
  );
}

export function GraphWorkspace() {
  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full">
      <ReactFlowProvider>
        <GraphView />
      </ReactFlowProvider>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/Header.tsx

import { useStore } from '../store/useStore';
import { Download, Image as ImageIcon, Type, Trash2, Figma, PanelLeftClose, PanelLeft, PanelRightClose, PanelRight, LayoutTemplate, Table } from 'lucide-react';
import { generateFigmaTranslation } from '../services/figmaTranslationService';

interface HeaderProps {
  onOpenTemplates: () => void;
  onToggleSpreadsheet: () => void;
  showSpreadsheet: boolean;
}

export function Header({ onOpenTemplates, onToggleSpreadsheet, showSpreadsheet }: HeaderProps) {
  const { mode, setMode, document, clearDocument, leftPanelOpen, rightPanelOpen, toggleLeftPanel, toggleRightPanel } = useStore();

  const handleExportJson = () => {
    if (!document) return;

    const serializeNode = (id: string): any => {
      const node = document.nodes[id];
      if (!node) return null;
      return {
        id: node.id,
        key: node.key,
        label: node.label,
        type: node.nodeType,
        metadata: node.metadata,
        children: node.children.map(serializeNode).filter(Boolean),
      };
    };

    const rootNodes = Object.values(document.nodes).filter(n => !n.parentId);
    const hierarchy = rootNodes.map(n => serializeNode(n.id));

    const exportData = {
      document_id: document.document_id,
      schema_version: document.schema_version,
      generated: new Date().toISOString(),
      hierarchy,
      spreadsheet: document.spreadsheetRows || {}
    };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = window.document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'hierarchy.json';
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleExportCsv = () => {
    if (!document) return;
    
    let csvContent = '';
    
    if (document.spreadsheetRows && Object.keys(document.spreadsheetRows).length > 0) {
      // Export spreadsheet rows
      const rows = Object.values(document.spreadsheetRows);
      const headers = [
        'row_id', 
        'control_name', 
        'function_summary', 
        'subsystem_ref', 
        'control_type', 
        'input_contract', 
        'output_contract', 
        'grouping_hint', 
        'priority', 
        'status', 
        'provenance'
      ];
      csvContent += headers.join(',') + '\n';
      
      rows.forEach(row => {
        const values = headers.map(header => {
          const val = (row as any)[header];
          if (val === null || val === undefined) return '""';
          return `"${String(val).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
        });
        csvContent += values.join(',') + '\n';
      });
    } else {
      // Export hierarchy nodes
      csvContent = 'id,key,label,type,parentId\n';
      Object.values(document.nodes).forEach(node => {
        csvContent += `"${node.id}","${node.key}","${node.label}","${node.nodeType}","${node.parentId || ''}"\n`;
      });
    }

    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = window.document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = document.spreadsheetRows && Object.keys(document.spreadsheetRows).length > 0 ? 'spreadsheet_export.csv' : 'hierarchy.csv';
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleExportFigma = () => {
    if (!document) return;
    
    const figmaTranslation = generateFigmaTranslation(document);
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(figmaTranslation, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = window.document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'figma_delivery.json';
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <header className="h-14 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 shrink-0 z-20 relative">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <button
          onClick={toggleLeftPanel}
          className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-md transition-colors"
          title={leftPanelOpen ? "Collapse Sidebar" : "Expand Sidebar"}
        >
          {leftPanelOpen ? <PanelLeftClose className="w-5 h-5" /> : <PanelLeft className="w-5 h-5" />}
        </button>
        <h1 className="font-semibold text-slate-800">UI Hierarchy Mapper</h1>
        <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-100 rounded-md p-1">
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('text')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-sm text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
              mode === 'text' ? 'bg-white shadow-sm text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Type className="w-4 h-4" />
            Text
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('image')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-sm text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
              mode === 'image' ? 'bg-white shadow-sm text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <ImageIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
            Image
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <button
          onClick={onToggleSpreadsheet}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium rounded-md transition-colors ${
            showSpreadsheet ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800' : 'text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100'
          }`}
        >
          <Table className="w-4 h-4" />
          Spreadsheet
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={onOpenTemplates}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-indigo-700 bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 rounded-md transition-colors mr-2"
        >
          <LayoutTemplate className="w-4 h-4" />
          Templates
        </button>
        {document && (
          <>
            <button
              onClick={clearDocument}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-md transition-colors"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              Clear
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={handleExportCsv}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-md transition-colors"
            >
              <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
              CSV
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={handleExportJson}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-md transition-colors"
            >
              <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
              JSON
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={handleExportFigma}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded-md transition-colors"
            >
              <Figma className="w-4 h-4" />
              Figma Delivery
            </button>
          </>
        )}
        <button
          onClick={toggleRightPanel}
          className="p-1.5 ml-2 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-md transition-colors"
          title={rightPanelOpen ? "Collapse Properties" : "Expand Properties"}
        >
          {rightPanelOpen ? <PanelRightClose className="w-5 h-5" /> : <PanelRight className="w-5 h-5" />}
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/PropertiesPanel.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store/useStore';
import { BaseNode, NODE_TYPES, NodeType } from '../types';
import { X, Trash2, Plus } from 'lucide-react';

const isDescendant = (nodes: Record<string, BaseNode>, parentId: string, childId: string): boolean => {
  const node = nodes[parentId];
  if (!node) return false;
  if (node.children.includes(childId)) return true;
  return node.children.some(id => isDescendant(nodes, id, childId));
};

export function PropertiesPanel() {
  const { document, selectNode, updateNode, deleteNode, addNode } = useStore();
  const selectedId = document?.layout?.selectedNode;
  const selectedNode = selectedId ? document?.nodes[selectedId] : null;

  if (!document || !selectedNode) {
    return (
      <aside className="w-full bg-white flex flex-col h-full shrink-0">
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider">Properties</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-sm text-slate-500 p-4 text-center">
          Select a node to view and edit its properties.
        </div>
      </aside>
    );
  }

  const handleLabelChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    updateNode(selectedNode.id, { label: e.target.value });
  };

  const handleTypeChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    updateNode(selectedNode.id, { nodeType: e.target.value as NodeType });
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    deleteNode(selectedNode.id);
  };

  const handleAddChild = () => {
    const idx = NODE_TYPES.indexOf(selectedNode.nodeType);
    const childType = idx < NODE_TYPES.length - 1 ? NODE_TYPES[idx + 1] : NODE_TYPES[NODE_TYPES.length - 1];
    addNode(selectedNode.id, childType, `New ${childType}`);
  };

  const associatedRow = document.spreadsheetRows ? 
    Object.values(document.spreadsheetRows).find(r => r.control_name === selectedNode.label || r.row_id === selectedNode.id) : null;

  return (
    <aside className="w-full bg-white flex flex-col h-full shrink-0 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between sticky top-0 bg-white z-10">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider">Properties</h2>
        <button onClick={() => selectNode(null)} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100">
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 space-y-6">
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Label</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={selectedNode.label}
              onChange={handleLabelChange}
              className="w-full p-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Type</label>
            <select
              value={selectedNode.nodeType}
              onChange={handleTypeChange}
              className="w-full p-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white"
            >
              {NODE_TYPES.map((type) => (
                <option key={type} value={type}>{type}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Parent</label>
            <select
              value={selectedNode.parentId || ''}
              onChange={(e) => useStore.getState().moveNode(selectedNode.id, e.target.value || null)}
              className="w-full p-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white"
            >
              <option value="">(None - Root)</option>
              {Object.values(document.nodes)
                .filter(n => n.id !== selectedNode.id && !isDescendant(document.nodes, selectedNode.id, n.id))
                .map(n => (
                  <option key={n.id} value={n.id}>{n.label} ({n.nodeType})</option>
                ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Machine Key</label>
            <div className="w-full p-2 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md text-slate-600 font-mono">
              {selectedNode.key}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">ID</label>
            <div className="w-full p-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md text-slate-400 font-mono truncate">
              {selectedNode.id}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {selectedNode.metadata && (
          <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-3">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider">Metadata</h3>
            {selectedNode.metadata.confidence !== undefined && (
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Confidence</span>
                <span className={`font-medium ${selectedNode.metadata.confidence < 0.7 ? 'text-amber-600' : 'text-emerald-600'}`}>
                  {Math.round(selectedNode.metadata.confidence * 100)}%
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
            {selectedNode.metadata.type_hint && (
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">AI Type Hint</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{selectedNode.metadata.type_hint}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {selectedNode.metadata.provenance && (
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Provenance</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700 capitalize">{selectedNode.metadata.provenance}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {selectedNode.metadata.unresolved_fields && selectedNode.metadata.unresolved_fields.length > 0 && (
              <div className="text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500 block mb-1">Unresolved Fields</span>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                  {selectedNode.metadata.unresolved_fields.map((field, i) => (
                    <span key={i} className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 rounded text-xs">
                      {field}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            {selectedNode.mergeMetadata && Object.keys(selectedNode.mergeMetadata).length > 0 && (
              <div className="text-sm mt-4">
                <span className="text-slate-500 block mb-2 font-semibold">Merge State</span>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {Object.entries(selectedNode.mergeMetadata).map(([field, meta]) => (
                    <div key={field} className={`p-2 rounded text-xs border ${meta.merge_state === 'CONFLICT' ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-200' : meta.merge_state === 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED' ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-200' : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200'}`}>
                      <div className="flex justify-between font-medium mb-1">
                        <span className="capitalize">{field}</span>
                        <span className={`${meta.merge_state === 'CONFLICT' ? 'text-red-600' : meta.merge_state === 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED' ? 'text-blue-600' : 'text-emerald-600'}`}>
                          {meta.merge_state.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {meta.conflict_reason && (
                        <div className="text-red-700 mt-1">Conflict: {meta.conflict_reason}</div>
                      )}
                      {meta.advisory_candidates && meta.advisory_candidates.length > 0 && (
                        <div className="text-slate-600 mt-1">Advisory: {meta.advisory_candidates.join(', ')}</div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {associatedRow && (
          <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-3">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider">Spreadsheet Data</h3>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex flex-col text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Function Summary</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-50 p-2 rounded border border-slate-100">{associatedRow.function_summary || 'N/A'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Subsystem Ref</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{associatedRow.subsystem_ref || 'N/A'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Control Type</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{associatedRow.control_type || 'N/A'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Input Contract</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{associatedRow.input_contract || 'N/A'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Output Contract</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{associatedRow.output_contract || 'N/A'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Grouping Hint</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{associatedRow.grouping_hint || 'N/A'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Status</span>
                <select
                  value={associatedRow.status}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const newStatus = e.target.value as any;
                    const spreadsheetRows = { ...document.spreadsheetRows };
                    const rowId = Object.keys(spreadsheetRows).find(key => spreadsheetRows[key].row_id === associatedRow.row_id);
                    if (rowId) {
                      spreadsheetRows[rowId] = { ...spreadsheetRows[rowId], status: newStatus };
                      useStore.getState().loadDocument({ ...document, spreadsheetRows });
                    }
                  }}
                  className={`text-xs font-semibold rounded px-2 py-1 outline-none ${
                    associatedRow.status === 'VALID' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700' :
                    associatedRow.status === 'VALID_WITH_WARNINGS' ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700' :
                    associatedRow.status === 'INVALID' ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700' :
                    associatedRow.status === 'CONFLICT' ? 'bg-orange-50 text-orange-700' :
                    associatedRow.status === 'RESOLVED' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' :
                    'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <option value="VALID">Valid</option>
                  <option value="VALID_WITH_WARNINGS">Warnings</option>
                  <option value="INVALID">Invalid</option>
                  <option value="CONFLICT">Conflict</option>
                  <option value="UNRESOLVED">Unresolved</option>
                  <option value="RESOLVED">Resolved</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-3">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider">Actions</h3>
          <button
            onClick={handleAddChild}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 px-4 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-blue-100 transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            Add Child Node
          </button>
          {selectedNode.children.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => useStore.getState().expandSubtree(selectedNode.id)}
                className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center py-2 px-4 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
              >
                Expand All
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => useStore.getState().collapseSubtree(selectedNode.id)}
                className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center py-2 px-4 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
              >
                Collapse All
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={handleDelete}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 px-4 bg-red-50 text-red-700 rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-red-100 transition-colors"
          >
            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
            Delete Node
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/Sidebar.tsx
------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store/useStore';
import { extractHierarchyFromText, extractHierarchyFromImage } from '../services/ai';
import { Loader2, Upload, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { NODE_TYPES, NodeType } from '../types';

export function Sidebar() {
  const { mode, document, loadDocument, addNode, selectNode } = useStore();
  const [textInput, setTextInput] = useState('');
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleProcessText = async () => {
    if (!textInput.trim()) return;
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const doc = await extractHierarchyFromText(textInput);
      loadDocument(doc);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to process text');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleProcessImage = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onloadend = async () => {
        const base64 = (reader.result as string).split(',')[1];
        const doc = await extractHierarchyFromImage(base64, file.type);
        loadDocument(doc);
        setIsProcessing(false);
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to process image');
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const [newNodeLabel, setNewNodeLabel] = useState('');
  const [newNodeType, setNewNodeType] = useState<NodeType>('System');

  const handleAddManualNode = () => {
    if (!newNodeLabel.trim()) return;
    addNode(null, newNodeType, newNodeLabel);
    setNewNodeLabel('');
  };

  return (
    <aside className="w-full bg-white flex flex-col h-full overflow-y-auto shrink-0">
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-3 uppercase tracking-wider">Ingestion</h2>
        
        {mode === 'text' ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <textarea
              value={textInput}
              onChange={(e) => setTextInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Describe the UI (e.g., 'A dashboard with a sidebar navigation, a top header, and a main content area containing a chart and a data table...')"
              className="w-full h-32 p-3 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 resize-none"
            />
            <button
              onClick={handleProcessText}
              disabled={isProcessing || !textInput.trim()}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 px-4 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
              {isProcessing && <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />}
              Process Text
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <label className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-full h-32 border-2 border-slate-300 border-dashed rounded-md cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors">
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center pt-5 pb-6">
                {isProcessing ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500 animate-spin mb-2" />
                ) : (
                  <Upload className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500 mb-2" />
                )}
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                  {isProcessing ? 'Processing image...' : 'Click or drag to upload'}
                </p>
              </div>
              <input type="file" className="hidden" accept="image/*" onChange={handleProcessImage} disabled={isProcessing} />
            </label>
          </div>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div className="mt-3 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm rounded-md border border-red-200">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {document && document.extraction_report && (
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">Extraction Report</h2>
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-600 space-y-1">
            <p>Nodes detected: <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{document.extraction_report.final_normalized_count}</span></p>
            {document.extraction_report.normalization_issues.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-2">
                <p className="font-medium text-amber-700">Warnings:</p>
                <ul className="list-disc pl-4 text-xs text-amber-600 space-y-1 mt-1">
                  {document.extraction_report.normalization_issues.map((issue, i) => (
                    <li key={i}>{issue}</li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </div>
            )}
            {document.extraction_report.detected_components.some(c => c.unresolved_fields && c.unresolved_fields.length > 0) && (
              <div className="mt-2">
                <p className="font-medium text-amber-700">Unresolved Fields (Truthful Failure):</p>
                <ul className="list-disc pl-4 text-xs text-amber-600 space-y-1 mt-1">
                  {document.extraction_report.detected_components
                    .filter(c => c.unresolved_fields && c.unresolved_fields.length > 0)
                    .map((c, i) => (
                      <li key={i}>{c.label}: {c.unresolved_fields?.join(', ')}</li>
                    ))}
                </ul>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="p-4">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-3 uppercase tracking-wider">Manual Add</h2>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={newNodeLabel}
            onChange={(e) => setNewNodeLabel(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Node Label"
            className="w-full p-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
          />
          <select
            value={newNodeType}
            onChange={(e) => setNewNodeType(e.target.value as NodeType)}
            className="w-full p-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white"
          >
            {NODE_TYPES.map((type) => (
              <option key={type} value={type}>{type}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button
            onClick={handleAddManualNode}
            disabled={!newNodeLabel.trim()}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 px-4 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-sm font-medium hover:bg-slate-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            Add Root Node
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SpreadsheetView.tsx
------
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store/useStore';
import { SpreadsheetRow } from '../types';
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, HelpCircle, XCircle, Search, Filter, Upload, Download } from 'lucide-react';

export const SpreadsheetView: React.FC = () => {
  const { document, selectNode, setFocusNode, importSpreadsheet } = useStore();
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleExportCsv = () => {
    if (!document || !document.spreadsheetRows) return;
    
    const rows = Object.values(document.spreadsheetRows);
    if (rows.length === 0) {
      alert("No data available to export.");
      return;
    }

    const headers = [
      'row_id', 
      'control_name', 
      'function_summary', 
      'subsystem_ref', 
      'control_type', 
      'input_contract', 
      'output_contract', 
      'grouping_hint', 
      'priority', 
      'status', 
      'provenance'
    ];

    let csvContent = headers.join(',') + '\n';
    
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const values = headers.map(header => {
        const val = (row as any)[header];
        if (val === null || val === undefined) return '""';
        return `"${String(val).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
      });
      csvContent += values.join(',') + '\n';
    });

    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = window.document.createElement('a');
    link.setAttribute('href', url);
    link.setAttribute('download', `lever_inventory_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`);
    link.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    window.document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    window.document.body.removeChild(link);
  };

  const handleFileUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      const csv = event.target?.result as string;
      if (!csv) return;

      const lines = csv.split('\n');
      const headers = lines[0].split(',').map(h => h.trim());
      
      const newRows: SpreadsheetRow[] = [];
      for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
        if (!lines[i].trim()) continue;
        const values = lines[i].split(',').map(v => v.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
        
        const row: any = {
          status: 'VALID',
          provenance: 'SPREADSHEET',
          import_timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
          source_file: file.name
        };

        headers.forEach((header, index) => {
          row[header] = values[index];
        });

        // Ensure required fields
        if (!row.row_id) row.row_id = `imported-${Date.now()}-${i}`;
        if (!row.control_name) row.control_name = `Unknown Control ${i}`;
        
        newRows.push(row as SpreadsheetRow);
      }

      const mode = window.confirm('Do you want to replace the current spreadsheet? (Cancel to merge)') ? 'replace' : 'merge';
      importSpreadsheet(newRows, mode);
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
  };

  if (!document) {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 text-slate-500">
        Load a document or template to view the spreadsheet.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const rows = Object.values(document.spreadsheetRows || {});

  const filteredRows = rows.filter(row => {
    const matchesSearch = 
      row.control_name?.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      row.function_summary?.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      row.subsystem_ref?.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());
    
    const matchesStatus = statusFilter === 'ALL' || row.status === statusFilter;

    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  const getStatusIcon = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'VALID': return <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" />;
      case 'VALID_WITH_WARNINGS': return <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />;
      case 'INVALID': return <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />;
      case 'CONFLICT': return <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-orange-500" />;
      case 'UNRESOLVED': return <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />;
      case 'RESOLVED': return <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500" />;
      default: return <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />;
    }
  };

  const handleRowClick = (row: SpreadsheetRow) => {
    const matchedNode = Object.values(document.nodes).find(n => 
      n.label === row.control_name || n.id === row.row_id
    );

    if (matchedNode) {
      selectNode(matchedNode.id);
      setFocusNode(matchedNode.id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-white border-t border-slate-200 shadow-lg z-20">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Lever Inventory</h2>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-2 py-1">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search controls..."
              value={searchTerm}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
              className="text-sm outline-none w-48"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
            {['ALL', 'VALID', 'VALID_WITH_WARNINGS', 'INVALID', 'CONFLICT', 'UNRESOLVED', 'RESOLVED'].map((status) => (
              <button
                key={status}
                onClick={() => setStatusFilter(status)}
                className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-all ${
                  statusFilter === status 
                    ? 'bg-white text-slate-800 shadow-sm' 
                    : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                {status === 'VALID_WITH_WARNINGS' ? 'Warnings' : status.charAt(0) + status.slice(1).toLowerCase()}
                <span className="ml-1.5 opacity-50">
                  ({rows.filter(r => status === 'ALL' || r.status === status).length})
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="h-4 w-px bg-slate-300 mx-1" />
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
            {filteredRows.length} displayed
          </div>
          <input
            type="file"
            accept=".csv"
            className="hidden"
            ref={fileInputRef}
            onChange={handleFileUpload}
          />
          <button
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-md transition-colors"
          >
            <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
            Import
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleExportCsv}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-white bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors shadow-sm"
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
            Export
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
          <thead className="bg-slate-50 sticky top-0 z-10 shadow-sm">
            <tr>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200 w-10">St</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Control Name</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Function Summary</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Subsystem</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Type</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Input Contract</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Output Contract</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Grouping Hint</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-slate-600 border-b border-slate-200">Provenance</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {filteredRows.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={9} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">
                  No rows found matching your criteria.
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              filteredRows.map((row) => (
                <tr 
                  key={row.row_id} 
                  onClick={() => handleRowClick(row)}
                  className="border-b border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <td className="px-4 py-2" title={row.status}>
                    {getStatusIcon(row.status)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 font-medium text-slate-800">{row.control_name}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-600 truncate max-w-xs" title={row.function_summary}>{row.function_summary}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-600">{row.subsystem_ref}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-600">
                    <span className="bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs">{row.control_type}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-500 font-mono text-xs">{row.input_contract || '-'}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-500 font-mono text-xs">{row.output_contract || '-'}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-600">{row.grouping_hint}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-wider uppercase text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                      {row.provenance}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/TemplateLibrary.tsx
------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store/useStore';
import { BUILT_IN_TEMPLATES } from '../data/templates';
import { X, LayoutTemplate, Plus, Replace } from 'lucide-react';

interface TemplateLibraryProps {
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const TemplateLibrary: React.FC<TemplateLibraryProps> = ({ onClose }) => {
  const { loadTemplate, document } = useStore();
  const [previewTemplate, setPreviewTemplate] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleLoad = (templateId: string, mode: 'replace' | 'insert') => {
    const template = BUILT_IN_TEMPLATES.find(t => t.id === templateId);
    if (template) {
      if (mode === 'replace' && document) {
        if (!window.confirm('This will clear your current workspace. Are you sure?')) {
          return;
        }
      }
      loadTemplate(template.document, mode);
      onClose();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-5xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <LayoutTemplate className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" />
            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-slate-800">Template Library</h2>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 rounded-full hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors">
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 bg-slate-50">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {BUILT_IN_TEMPLATES.map((template) => (
              <div key={template.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <div className="p-5 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-indigo-600 uppercase bg-indigo-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">
                      {template.family}
                    </span>
                    <span className={`text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded-md ${
                      template.variant === 'SIMPLE' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700'
                    }`}>
                      {template.variant}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-2">{template.name}</h3>
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 mb-4 line-clamp-2">{template.description}</p>
                  
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-4">
                    <div className="bg-slate-50 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1">Nodes</div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{template.estimatedNodes}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-slate-50 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1">Rows</div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{template.estimatedRows}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Teaches:</div>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                      {template.teachesPatterns.map((pattern, idx) => (
                        <span key={idx} className="text-xs bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                          {pattern}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleLoad(template.id, 'replace')}
                    className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 px-3 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-indigo-700 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <Replace className="w-4 h-4" />
                    Load New
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleLoad(template.id, 'insert')}
                    disabled={!document}
                    className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 px-3 bg-white text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    title={!document ? "Open a workspace first to insert" : "Insert into current workspace"}
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                    Insert
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/data/templates.ts

import { HierarchyDocument, BaseNode, SpreadsheetRow, TemplateMetadata } from '../types';

export interface TemplateDefinition {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  family: string;
  variant: 'SIMPLE' | 'EXPANDED';
  description: string;
  complexity: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
  estimatedNodes: number;
  estimatedRows: number;
  bestUsedFor: string;
  teachesPatterns: string[];
  lastUpdated: string;
  document: HierarchyDocument;
}

const createBaseDocument = (id: string, title: string, metadata: TemplateMetadata): HierarchyDocument => ({
  document_id: id,
  document_type: 'UIHierarchy',
  schema_version: '1.0',
  title,
  extraction_source: 'template',
  templateMetadata: metadata,
  metadata: { generated: new Date().toISOString() },
  nodes: {},
  spreadsheetRows: {},
  layout: {
    nodePositions: {},
    camera: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 },
    selectedNode: null,
    expandedNodes: [],
  },
});

import { GoogleGenAI, Type } from '@google/genai';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import { BaseNode, ExtractionReport, HierarchyDocument, NodeType, TempTemplate, DocumentInputs } from '../types';
import { runHierarchyOrganizer } from './hierarchyOrganizer';

const ai = new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey: process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY });

const schema = {
  type: Type.OBJECT,
  properties: {
    components: {
      type: Type.ARRAY,
      items: {
        type: Type.OBJECT,
        properties: {
          label: { type: Type.STRING },
          type_hint: { type: Type.STRING, enum: ["System", "Zone", "Module", "Container", "Control"] },
          confidence: { type: Type.NUMBER },
          parent_label: { type: Type.STRING, description: "The label of the parent component, or null if root" }
        },
        required: ["label", "type_hint", "confidence"]
      }
    },
    normalization_issues: {
      type: Type.ARRAY,
      items: { type: Type.STRING }
    }
  },
  required: ["components", "normalization_issues"]
};

export const extractHierarchyFromText = async (text: string): Promise<HierarchyDocument> => {
  const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
    model: 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview',
    contents: `Analyze the following UI description and extract a strict 5-layer hierarchy: System -> Zone -> Module -> Container -> Control.
Description:
${text}`,
    config: {
      responseMimeType: 'application/json',
      responseSchema: schema,
      systemInstruction: "You are a UI architect. Extract components and strictly map them to the 5-layer hierarchy. Ensure every component has a valid parent_label except the root System. The root System should represent the entire app/screen. Zones are major sections. Modules are functional groups. Containers are UI panels. Controls are buttons/inputs."
    }
  });

  const data = JSON.parse(response.text || '{}');
  return normalizeToDocument(data, 'text', text);
};

const imageSchema = {
  type: Type.OBJECT,
  properties: {
    hierarchyTemp: {
      type: Type.ARRAY,
      items: {
        type: Type.OBJECT,
        properties: {
          label: { type: Type.STRING },
          type_hint: { type: Type.STRING, enum: ["System", "Zone", "Module", "Container", "Control", "Unknown", "Not enough data", "Unclear"] },
          confidence: { type: Type.NUMBER },
          parent_label: { type: Type.STRING, description: "The label of the parent component, or 'Unresolved grouping' if unclear" },
          unresolved_fields: { type: Type.ARRAY, items: { type: Type.STRING } }
        },
        required: ["label", "type_hint", "confidence"]
      }
    },
    spreadsheetTemp: {
      type: Type.ARRAY,
      items: {
        type: Type.OBJECT,
        properties: {
          control_name: { type: Type.STRING },
          function_summary: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Use 'Unknown' or 'Not enough data' if not visible" },
          control_type: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Use 'Control type not inferable' if unclear" },
          grouping_hint: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Use 'Unresolved grouping' if unclear" },
          related_controls: { type: Type.ARRAY, items: { type: Type.STRING } },
          input_contract: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Use 'IO not inferable' if unclear" },
          output_contract: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Use 'IO not inferable' if unclear" },
          confidence: { type: Type.NUMBER },
          unresolved_fields: { type: Type.ARRAY, items: { type: Type.STRING } }
        },
        required: ["control_name", "function_summary", "control_type", "grouping_hint", "confidence"]
      }
    },
    normalization_issues: {
      type: Type.ARRAY,
      items: { type: Type.STRING }
    }
  },
  required: ["hierarchyTemp", "spreadsheetTemp", "normalization_issues"]
};

export const extractHierarchyFromImage = async (base64Image: string, mimeType: string): Promise<HierarchyDocument> => {
  const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
    model: 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview',
    contents: {
      parts: [
        { inlineData: { data: base64Image, mimeType } },
        { text: "Analyze this UI screenshot and extract probable interface structure and exposed handles. Follow the Truthful Failure Rule: if you cannot directly justify a field from visible evidence, fail gracefully. Do not fabricate certainty. Use acceptable unresolved values: 'Unknown', 'Not enough data', 'Unclear', 'Unresolved grouping', 'IO not inferable', 'Control type not inferable'." }
      ]
    },
    config: {
      responseMimeType: 'application/json',
      responseSchema: imageSchema,
      systemInstruction: "You are a UI architect. Extract components into a temporary template (hierarchy and spreadsheet). TRUTHFUL FAILURE RULE: Never fabricate certainty. If evidence is lacking, use 'Unknown', 'Not enough data', 'Unclear', 'Unresolved grouping', 'IO not inferable', or 'Control type not inferable'. Mark fields as unresolved in unresolved_fields array if you are guessing."
    }
  });

  const data = JSON.parse(response.text || '{}');
  return normalizeImageToDocument(data, 'image', 'Screenshot');
};

const normalizeImageToDocument = (data: any, source: 'image', rawInput: string): HierarchyDocument => {
  const nodes: Record<string, BaseNode> = {};
  const spreadsheetRows: Record<string, any> = {};
  const hierarchyTemp = data.hierarchyTemp || [];
  const spreadsheetTemp = data.spreadsheetTemp || [];
  
  const labelToId: Record<string, string> = {};
  
  hierarchyTemp.forEach((comp: any) => {
    const id = uuidv4();
    labelToId[comp.label] = id;
    
    let nodeType = comp.type_hint;
    if (!["System", "Zone", "Module", "Container", "Control"].includes(nodeType)) {
      nodeType = "Control"; // Fallback for unresolved
    }

    nodes[id] = {
      id,
      key: comp.label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/(^_|_$)/g, ''),
      label: comp.label,
      nodeType: nodeType as NodeType,
      parentId: null,
      children: [],
      metadata: {
        confidence: comp.confidence,
        type_hint: comp.type_hint,
        provenance: 'inferred',
        unresolved_fields: comp.unresolved_fields || []
      }
    };
  });

  hierarchyTemp.forEach((comp: any) => {
    const id = labelToId[comp.label];
    if (comp.parent_label && comp.parent_label !== 'Unresolved grouping' && labelToId[comp.parent_label]) {
      const parentId = labelToId[comp.parent_label];
      nodes[id].parentId = parentId;
      if (nodes[parentId]) {
        nodes[parentId].children.push(id);
      }
    }
  });

  spreadsheetTemp.forEach((row: any) => {
    const id = uuidv4();
    spreadsheetRows[id] = {
      row_id: id,
      control_name: row.control_name,
      function_summary: row.function_summary,
      subsystem_ref: 'Unknown',
      control_type: row.control_type,
      grouping_hint: row.grouping_hint,
      related_controls: row.related_controls || [],
      input_contract: row.input_contract || null,
      output_contract: row.output_contract || null,
      priority: 'Medium',
      status: 'VALID',
      confidence: row.confidence,
      source_reference: rawInput,
      provenance: 'inferred',
      unresolved_fields: row.unresolved_fields || []
    };
  });

  const report: ExtractionReport = {
    raw_input: rawInput,
    detected_components: hierarchyTemp.map((c: any) => ({
      label: c.label,
      type_hint: c.type_hint,
      confidence: c.confidence,
      unresolved_fields: c.unresolved_fields
    })),
    normalization_issues: data.normalization_issues || [],
    final_normalized_count: Object.keys(nodes).length
  };

  const inputs: DocumentInputs = {
    spreadsheetTruth: {},
    hierarchyStructure: {},
    screenshotTemplate: {
      hierarchyTemp: Object.values(nodes),
      spreadsheetTemp: Object.values(spreadsheetRows)
    }
  };

  const merged = runHierarchyOrganizer(inputs);

  return {
    document_id: uuidv4(),
    document_type: "UIHierarchy",
    schema_version: "1.0",
    inputs,
    nodes: merged.nodes,
    spreadsheetRows: merged.spreadsheetRows,
    extraction_source: source,
    metadata: {
      generated: new Date().toISOString()
    },
    extraction_report: report,
    layout: {
      nodePositions: {},
      camera: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 },
      selectedNode: null,
      expandedNodes: []
    }
  };
};

const normalizeToDocument = (data: any, source: 'text' | 'image', rawInput: string): HierarchyDocument => {
  const nodes: Record<string, BaseNode> = {};
  const components = data.components || [];
  
  const labelToId: Record<string, string> = {};
  
  components.forEach((comp: any) => {
    const id = uuidv4();
    labelToId[comp.label] = id;
    nodes[id] = {
      id,
      key: comp.label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/(^_|_$)/g, ''),
      label: comp.label,
      nodeType: comp.type_hint as NodeType,
      parentId: null,
      children: [],
      metadata: {
        confidence: comp.confidence,
        type_hint: comp.type_hint
      }
    };
  });

  components.forEach((comp: any) => {
    const id = labelToId[comp.label];
    if (comp.parent_label && labelToId[comp.parent_label]) {
      const parentId = labelToId[comp.parent_label];
      nodes[id].parentId = parentId;
      if (nodes[parentId]) {
        nodes[parentId].children.push(id);
      }
    }
  });

  const report: ExtractionReport = {
    raw_input: rawInput,
    detected_components: components,
    normalization_issues: data.normalization_issues || [],
    final_normalized_count: Object.keys(nodes).length
  };

  const inputs: DocumentInputs = {
    spreadsheetTruth: {},
    hierarchyStructure: nodes,
    screenshotTemplate: null
  };

  const merged = runHierarchyOrganizer(inputs);

  return {
    document_id: uuidv4(),
    document_type: "UIHierarchy",
    schema_version: "1.0",
    inputs,
    nodes: merged.nodes,
    spreadsheetRows: merged.spreadsheetRows,
    extraction_source: source,
    metadata: {
      generated: new Date().toISOString()
    },
    extraction_report: report,
    layout: {
      nodePositions: {},
      camera: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 },
      selectedNode: null,
      expandedNodes: []
    }
  };
};
