FILE: src/services/figmaTranslationService.ts
------
import { HierarchyDocument, FigmaTranslationObject, BaseNode, SpreadsheetRow, FigmaControlDetails } from '../types';

export const generateFigmaTranslation = (
  document: HierarchyDocument,
  exportMode: 'GROUPED_FRAME_EXPORT' | 'CONTROL_SURFACE_EXPORT' | 'WIREFLOW_EXPORT' = 'GROUPED_FRAME_EXPORT'
): FigmaTranslationObject[] => {
  const translations: FigmaTranslationObject[] = [];
  const { nodes, spreadsheetRows = {} } = document;

  // Group by Zone/Module from hierarchy, or grouping_zone from spreadsheet
  const groups: Record<string, {
    label: string;
    zoneId?: string;
    moduleId?: string;
    containerId?: string;
    controlIds: Set<string>;
    spreadsheetRefs: Set<string>;
    unresolvedFields: Set<string>;
    inputs: Set<string>;
    outputs: Set<string>;
    relatedControls: Set<string>;
    controls: FigmaControlDetails[];
  }> = {};

  // Helper to find ancestors
  const getAncestors = (nodeId: string) => {
    let current = nodes[nodeId];
    let zoneId, moduleId, containerId;
    let groupLabel = 'Unknown Group';

    while (current && current.parentId) {
      const parent = nodes[current.parentId];
      if (!parent) break;
      if (parent.nodeType === 'Container') containerId = parent.id;
      if (parent.nodeType === 'Module') {
        moduleId = parent.id;
        groupLabel = parent.label;
      }
      if (parent.nodeType === 'Zone') {
        zoneId = parent.id;
        groupLabel = parent.label; // Zone overrides Module for label if both exist
      }
      current = parent;
    }
    return { zoneId, moduleId, containerId, groupLabel };
  };

  // 1. Process Hierarchy Controls
  Object.values(nodes).forEach(node => {
    if (node.nodeType === 'Control') {
      const { zoneId, moduleId, containerId, groupLabel } = getAncestors(node.id);
      const groupKey = zoneId || moduleId || containerId || 'unresolved_hierarchy';
      
      if (!groups[groupKey]) {
        groups[groupKey] = {
          label: groupLabel,
          zoneId, moduleId, containerId,
          controlIds: new Set(),
          spreadsheetRefs: new Set(),
          unresolvedFields: new Set(),
          inputs: new Set(), outputs: new Set(), relatedControls: new Set(),
          controls: []
        };
      }
      
      groups[groupKey].controlIds.add(node.id);
      
      // Try to find matching spreadsheet row
      const row = Object.values(spreadsheetRows).find(r => r.row_id === node.id || r.control_name === node.label);
      
      groups[groupKey].controls.push({
        id: node.id,
        label: node.label,
        function_summary: row?.function_summary,
        subsystem_ref: row?.subsystem_ref,
        control_type: row?.control_type || node.metadata?.type_hint,
        input_contract: row?.input_contract || undefined,
        output_contract: row?.output_contract || undefined,
        grouping_hint: row?.grouping_hint,
        priority: row?.priority
      });

      if (node.metadata?.unresolved_fields) {
        node.metadata.unresolved_fields.forEach(f => groups[groupKey].unresolvedFields.add(f));
      }
    }
  });

  // 2. Process Spreadsheet Rows and Merge
  Object.values(spreadsheetRows).forEach(row => {
    // Try to match with hierarchy group by name or zone
    let matchedGroupKey = null;
    
    // Simple heuristic: if row.grouping_hint matches a group label
    for (const [key, group] of Object.entries(groups)) {
      if (row.grouping_hint && group.label.toLowerCase() === row.grouping_hint.toLowerCase()) {
        matchedGroupKey = key;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!matchedGroupKey) {
      matchedGroupKey = `spreadsheet_${row.row_id}`;
      groups[matchedGroupKey] = {
        label: row.grouping_hint || 'Unknown Group',
        controlIds: new Set(),
        spreadsheetRefs: new Set(),
        unresolvedFields: new Set(),
        inputs: new Set(), outputs: new Set(), relatedControls: new Set(),
        controls: []
      };
    }

    const group = groups[matchedGroupKey];
    group.spreadsheetRefs.add(row.row_id);
    if (row.input_contract) group.inputs.add(row.input_contract);
    if (row.output_contract) group.outputs.add(row.output_contract);
    if (row.related_controls) row.related_controls.forEach(r => group.relatedControls.add(r));
    
    // Add to controls array if not already added by hierarchy processing
    if (!group.controls.some(c => c.id === row.row_id || c.label === row.control_name)) {
      group.controls.push({
        id: row.row_id,
        label: row.control_name,
        function_summary: row.function_summary,
        subsystem_ref: row.subsystem_ref,
        control_type: row.control_type,
        input_contract: row.input_contract || undefined,
        output_contract: row.output_contract || undefined,
        grouping_hint: row.grouping_hint,
        priority: row.priority
      });
    }

    if (row.validation_errors) {
      row.validation_errors.forEach(err => group.unresolvedFields.add(err.field));
    }
  });

  // 3. Generate Translation Objects
  Object.entries(groups).forEach(([key, group], index) => {
    const isUnresolved = group.label === 'Unknown Group' || group.label === 'Unresolved grouping';
    
    const ioSummary = Array.from(group.inputs).concat(Array.from(group.outputs)).join(', ') || 'IO not inferable';
    const navSummary = Array.from(group.relatedControls).join(', ') || 'Unknown';

    translations.push({
      figma_group_id: `figma_grp_${index}_${Date.now()}`,
      figma_group_label: group.label,
      source_zone_id: group.zoneId,
      source_module_id: group.moduleId,
      source_container_id: group.containerId,
      source_control_ids: Array.from(group.controlIds),
      spreadsheet_row_refs: Array.from(group.spreadsheetRefs),
      controls: group.controls,
      interface_role: isUnresolved ? 'Unknown' : 'Grouped Control Surface',
      grouping_reason: isUnresolved ? 'Unresolved' : 'Hierarchy and Spreadsheet Mapping',
      io_binding_summary: ioSummary,
      navigation_binding_summary: navSummary,
      provenance: 'merged',
      unresolved_fields: Array.from(group.unresolvedFields),
      
      hierarchy_ref: group.zoneId || group.moduleId || group.containerId || 'unknown',
      spreadsheet_ref: Array.from(group.spreadsheetRefs).join(','),
      provenance_type: 'merged',
      export_mode: exportMode,
      confidence: isUnresolved ? 0.5 : 1.0,
      unresolved_count: group.unresolvedFields.size
    });
  });

  return translations;
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/services/hierarchyOrganizer.ts
------
import { BaseNode, SpreadsheetRow, DocumentInputs, MergeState, MergeMetadata, NodeType } from '../types';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';

export interface OrganizerOutput {
  nodes: Record<string, BaseNode>;
  spreadsheetRows: Record<string, SpreadsheetRow>;
  diagnostics: {
    ungroupedControls: string[];
    conflicts: string[];
    unresolvedPlacements: string[];
  };
}

export const runHierarchyOrganizer = (inputs: DocumentInputs, strictMode: boolean = true): OrganizerOutput => {
  const mergedNodes: Record<string, BaseNode> = {};
  const mergedRows: Record<string, SpreadsheetRow> = {};
  const diagnostics = {
    ungroupedControls: [] as string[],
    conflicts: [] as string[],
    unresolvedPlacements: [] as string[]
  };

  // 1. Ingest spreadsheet truth (Tier 1 - Authoritative)
  const truthRows = Object.values(inputs.spreadsheetTruth || {});
  
  // 2. Ingest hierarchy structure (Tier 2 - Template/Starter)
  const structureNodes = Object.values(inputs.hierarchyStructure || {});

  // 3. Ingest screenshot template suggestions (Tier 3 - Advisory)
  const screenNodes = inputs.screenshotTemplate?.hierarchyTemp || [];
  const screenRows = inputs.screenshotTemplate?.spreadsheetTemp || [];

  // Map screenshot rows by control name for matching
  const screenRowsByName = new Map(screenRows.map(r => [r.control_name.toLowerCase(), r]));
  
  // Process truth rows
  truthRows.forEach(truthRow => {
    const sRow = screenRowsByName.get(truthRow.control_name.toLowerCase());
    
    const mergedRow: SpreadsheetRow = { ...truthRow };
    mergedRow.mergeMetadata = {};

    if (sRow) {
      // Compare fields and detect conflicts
      const compareField = (field: keyof SpreadsheetRow, isArray: boolean = false) => {
        const tVal = truthRow[field];
        const sVal = sRow[field];
        
        let conflict = false;
        if (isArray) {
          const tArr = (tVal as string[]) || [];
          const sArr = (sVal as string[]) || [];
          if (sArr.length > 0 && sArr[0] !== 'IO not inferable' && tArr.join(',') !== sArr.join(',')) {
            conflict = true;
          }
        } else {
          if (sVal && sVal !== 'Unknown' && sVal !== 'Not enough data' && sVal !== 'Unclear' && sVal !== 'Unresolved grouping' && sVal !== 'Control type not inferable' && tVal !== sVal) {
            conflict = true;
          }
        }

        if (conflict) {
          mergedRow.mergeMetadata![field as string] = {
            source_priority: 1,
            source_refs: [truthRow.row_id, sRow.row_id],
            merge_state: 'CONFLICT',
            conflict_reason: `${field} mismatch`,
            advisory_candidates: [sVal]
          };
          diagnostics.conflicts.push(`Row ${truthRow.row_id}: ${field} mismatch`);
        } else {
          mergedRow.mergeMetadata![field as string] = {
            source_priority: 1,
            source_refs: [truthRow.row_id],
            merge_state: 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED'
          };
        }
      };

      compareField('function_summary');
      compareField('control_type');
      compareField('grouping_hint');
      compareField('input_contract');
      compareField('output_contract');
      compareField('related_controls', true);

      mergedRow.mergeMetadata['control_name'] = {
        source_priority: 1,
        source_refs: [truthRow.row_id],
        merge_state: 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED'
      };

      screenRowsByName.delete(truthRow.control_name.toLowerCase());
    } else {
      // No screenshot suggestion
      ['control_name', 'function_summary', 'control_type', 'grouping_hint', 'input_contract', 'output_contract', 'related_controls'].forEach(field => {
        mergedRow.mergeMetadata![field] = {
          source_priority: 1,
          source_refs: [truthRow.row_id],
          merge_state: 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED'
        };
      });
    }
    
    mergedRows[mergedRow.row_id] = mergedRow;
  });

  // Process remaining screenshot rows (inferred)
  screenRowsByName.forEach(sRow => {
    const mergedRow: SpreadsheetRow = { ...sRow };
    mergedRow.mergeMetadata = {};
    ['control_name', 'function_summary', 'control_type', 'grouping_hint', 'input_contract', 'output_contract', 'related_controls'].forEach(field => {
      mergedRow.mergeMetadata![field] = {
        source_priority: 3,
        source_refs: [sRow.row_id],
        merge_state: 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED'
      };
    });
    mergedRows[mergedRow.row_id] = mergedRow;
  });

  // Process hierarchy nodes
  const screenNodesByLabel = new Map(screenNodes.map(n => [n.label.toLowerCase(), n]));

  structureNodes.forEach(node => {
    const sNode = screenNodesByLabel.get(node.label.toLowerCase());
    const mergedNode: BaseNode = { ...node };
    mergedNode.mergeMetadata = {};

    if (sNode) {
      // Compare parent
      if (node.parentId !== sNode.parentId && sNode.parentId) {
        mergedNode.mergeMetadata['parentId'] = {
          source_priority: 2,
          source_refs: [node.id, sNode.id],
          merge_state: 'CONFLICT',
          conflict_reason: 'grouping mismatch',
          advisory_candidates: [sNode.parentId]
        };
        diagnostics.conflicts.push(`Node ${node.id}: grouping mismatch`);
      } else {
        mergedNode.mergeMetadata['parentId'] = {
          source_priority: 2,
          source_refs: [node.id],
          merge_state: 'DERIVED_CONFIRMED'
        };
      }

      mergedNode.mergeMetadata['label'] = {
        source_priority: 2,
        source_refs: [node.id],
        merge_state: 'DERIVED_CONFIRMED'
      };

      screenNodesByLabel.delete(node.label.toLowerCase());
    } else {
      ['label', 'parentId', 'nodeType'].forEach(field => {
        mergedNode.mergeMetadata![field] = {
          source_priority: 2,
          source_refs: [node.id],
          merge_state: 'DERIVED_CONFIRMED'
        };
      });
    }
    
    if (mergedNode.nodeType === 'Control' && !mergedNode.parentId) {
      diagnostics.ungroupedControls.push(mergedNode.id);
    }
    
    mergedNodes[mergedNode.id] = mergedNode;

    // Ensure bidirectional consistency: if node is a Control, it must have a SpreadsheetRow
    if (mergedNode.nodeType === 'Control' && !mergedRows[mergedNode.id]) {
      // Create a default row for manual/hierarchy-only controls
      mergedRows[mergedNode.id] = {
        row_id: mergedNode.id,
        control_name: mergedNode.label,
        function_summary: 'Manually added or structure-only control',
        subsystem_ref: 'Unknown',
        control_type: mergedNode.metadata?.type_hint || 'Control',
        input_contract: null,
        output_contract: null,
        grouping_hint: '',
        priority: 'Medium',
        status: 'UNRESOLVED',
        provenance: mergedNode.metadata?.provenance || 'USER_EDITED_FROM_TEMPLATE',
        mergeMetadata: {
          control_name: {
            source_priority: 2,
            source_refs: [mergedNode.id],
            merge_state: 'DERIVED_CONFIRMED'
          }
        }
      };
    }
  });

  // Process remaining screenshot nodes
  screenNodesByLabel.forEach(sNode => {
    const mergedNode: BaseNode = { ...sNode };
    mergedNode.mergeMetadata = {};
    ['label', 'parentId', 'nodeType'].forEach(field => {
      mergedNode.mergeMetadata![field] = {
        source_priority: 3,
        source_refs: [sNode.id],
        merge_state: 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED'
      };
    });
    
    if (mergedNode.nodeType === 'Control' && !mergedNode.parentId) {
      diagnostics.ungroupedControls.push(mergedNode.id);
    }
    
    mergedNodes[mergedNode.id] = mergedNode;

    // Ensure screenshot nodes also have rows if they are Controls
    if (mergedNode.nodeType === 'Control' && !mergedRows[mergedNode.id]) {
        mergedRows[mergedNode.id] = {
          row_id: mergedNode.id,
          control_name: mergedNode.label,
          function_summary: 'Inferred from screenshot template',
          subsystem_ref: 'Unknown',
          control_type: mergedNode.metadata?.type_hint || 'Control',
          input_contract: null,
          output_contract: null,
          grouping_hint: '',
          priority: 'Medium',
          status: 'UNRESOLVED',
          provenance: 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED',
          mergeMetadata: {
            control_name: {
              source_priority: 3,
              source_refs: [mergedNode.id],
              merge_state: 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED'
            }
          }
        };
      }
  });

  // Ensure all spreadsheet rows have a corresponding node in the hierarchy
  Object.values(mergedRows).forEach(row => {
    const existingNode = Object.values(mergedNodes).find(n => n.id === row.row_id || n.label === row.control_name);
    if (!existingNode) {
      // Create a node for this row
      const newNodeId = row.row_id;
      mergedNodes[newNodeId] = {
        id: newNodeId,
        nodeType: 'Control',
        label: row.control_name,
        key: row.control_name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_'),
        parentId: null, // Ungrouped by default
        children: [],
        metadata: {
          provenance: row.provenance,
          confidence: row.confidence || 1.0,
          type_hint: row.control_type
        },
        mergeMetadata: {
          label: {
            source_priority: 1,
            source_refs: [row.row_id],
            merge_state: 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED'
          }
        }
      };
      diagnostics.ungroupedControls.push(newNodeId);
    }
  });

  // Grouping Engine Logic: Try to resolve ungrouped controls based on grouping_hint
  diagnostics.ungroupedControls.forEach(controlId => {
    const controlNode = mergedNodes[controlId];
    if (!controlNode) return;

    // Find corresponding row
    const row = Object.values(mergedRows).find(r => r.row_id === controlId || r.control_name === controlNode.label);
    if (row && row.grouping_hint) {
      // Try to find a matching group node
      const groupNode = Object.values(mergedNodes).find(n => 
        n.nodeType !== 'Control' && 
        (n.label.toLowerCase() === row.grouping_hint.toLowerCase() || 
         n.key === row.grouping_hint.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_'))
      );

      if (groupNode) {
        // Assign to group
        controlNode.parentId = groupNode.id;
        if (!groupNode.children.includes(controlId)) {
          groupNode.children.push(controlId);
        }
        
        controlNode.mergeMetadata = controlNode.mergeMetadata || {};
        controlNode.mergeMetadata['parentId'] = {
          source_priority: 1,
          source_refs: [row.row_id],
          merge_state: 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED'
        };
        
        // Remove from ungrouped
        diagnostics.ungroupedControls = diagnostics.ungroupedControls.filter(id => id !== controlId);
      } else if (!strictMode) {
        // In normal mode, create the group if it doesn't exist
        const newGroupId = uuidv4();
        mergedNodes[newGroupId] = {
          id: newGroupId,
          nodeType: 'Container', // Default to Container
          label: row.grouping_hint,
          key: row.grouping_hint.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_'),
          parentId: null,
          children: [controlId],
          metadata: {
            provenance: 'DERIVED_CONFIRMED',
            confidence: 1.0
          },
          mergeMetadata: {
            label: {
              source_priority: 1,
              source_refs: [row.row_id],
              merge_state: 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED'
            }
          }
        };
        
        controlNode.parentId = newGroupId;
        controlNode.mergeMetadata = controlNode.mergeMetadata || {};
        controlNode.mergeMetadata['parentId'] = {
          source_priority: 1,
          source_refs: [row.row_id],
          merge_state: 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED'
        };
        
        diagnostics.ungroupedControls = diagnostics.ungroupedControls.filter(id => id !== controlId);
      } else {
        diagnostics.unresolvedPlacements.push(controlId);
      }
    } else {
      diagnostics.unresolvedPlacements.push(controlId);
    }
  });

  return { nodes: mergedNodes, spreadsheetRows: mergedRows, diagnostics };
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/store/useStore.ts
------
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { persist } from 'zustand/middleware';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import { BaseNode, HierarchyDocument, NodeType, NODE_TYPES, SpreadsheetRow, DocumentInputs } from '../types';
import { runHierarchyOrganizer } from '../services/hierarchyOrganizer';
import dagre from 'dagre';

export const getVisibleNodes = (nodes: Record<string, BaseNode>, collapsedNodes: Record<string, boolean>, focusNodeId: string | null): Set<string> => {
  const visible = new Set<string>();
  
  const focusPath = new Set<string>();
  const strictAncestors = new Set<string>();
  if (focusNodeId && nodes[focusNodeId]) {
    let curr = focusNodeId;
    while (curr) {
      focusPath.add(curr);
      if (curr !== focusNodeId) {
        strictAncestors.add(curr);
      }
      curr = nodes[curr].parentId || '';
    }
  }

  const traverse = (nodeId: string, isUnderFocus: boolean) => {
    const node = nodes[nodeId];
    if (!node) return;

    const isFocusPath = focusPath.has(nodeId);
    const isFocused = nodeId === focusNodeId;
    const isVisibleInFocus = !focusNodeId || isFocusPath || isUnderFocus;

    if (isVisibleInFocus) {
      visible.add(nodeId);
      
      const isCollapsed = collapsedNodes[nodeId];
      const isStrictAncestor = strictAncestors.has(nodeId);
      if (!isCollapsed || isStrictAncestor) {
        node.children.forEach(childId => {
          traverse(childId, isUnderFocus || isFocused);
        });
      }
    }
  };

  const rootIds = Object.values(nodes).filter(n => !n.parentId).map(n => n.id);
  rootIds.forEach(id => traverse(id, false));

  return visible;
};

interface AppState {
  document: HierarchyDocument | null;
  mode: 'text' | 'image';
  leftPanelOpen: boolean;
  rightPanelOpen: boolean;
  collapsedNodes: Record<string, boolean>;
  focusNodeId: string | null;
  setMode: (mode: 'text' | 'image') => void;
  toggleLeftPanel: () => void;
  toggleRightPanel: () => void;
  toggleNodeCollapse: (id: string) => void;
  expandSubtree: (id: string) => void;
  collapseSubtree: (id: string) => void;
  setFocusNode: (id: string | null) => void;
  loadDocument: (doc: HierarchyDocument) => void;
  loadTemplate: (template: HierarchyDocument, mode: 'replace' | 'insert') => void;
  importSpreadsheet: (rows: SpreadsheetRow[], mode: 'replace' | 'merge') => void;
  addNode: (parentId: string | null, nodeType: NodeType, label: string) => void;
  updateNode: (id: string, updates: Partial<BaseNode>) => void;
  deleteNode: (id: string) => void;
  moveNode: (id: string, newParentId: string | null) => void;
  selectNode: (id: string | null) => void;
  autoLayout: () => void;
  clearDocument: () => void;
  updateNodePosition: (id: string, position: { x: number; y: number }) => void;
}

const generateMachineKey = (label: string) => {
  return label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/(^_|_$)/g, '');
};

const getValidChildType = (parentType: NodeType | null): NodeType | null => {
  if (!parentType) return 'System';
  const idx = NODE_TYPES.indexOf(parentType);
  if (idx >= 0 && idx < NODE_TYPES.length - 1) {
    return NODE_TYPES[idx + 1];
  }
  return null;
};

export const useStore = create<AppState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      document: null,
      mode: 'text',
      leftPanelOpen: true,
      rightPanelOpen: true,
      collapsedNodes: {},
      focusNodeId: null,
      setMode: (mode) => set({ mode }),
      toggleLeftPanel: () => set((state) => ({ leftPanelOpen: !state.leftPanelOpen })),
      toggleRightPanel: () => set((state) => ({ rightPanelOpen: !state.rightPanelOpen })),
      toggleNodeCollapse: (id) => {
        set((state) => ({
          collapsedNodes: {
            ...state.collapsedNodes,
            [id]: !state.collapsedNodes[id],
          },
        }));
        get().autoLayout();
      },
      expandSubtree: (id) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          const newCollapsed = { ...state.collapsedNodes };
          const traverse = (nodeId: string) => {
            newCollapsed[nodeId] = false;
            state.document!.nodes[nodeId]?.children.forEach(traverse);
          };
          traverse(id);
          return { collapsedNodes: newCollapsed };
        });
        get().autoLayout();
      },
      collapseSubtree: (id) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          const newCollapsed = { ...state.collapsedNodes };
          const traverse = (nodeId: string) => {
            if (state.document!.nodes[nodeId]?.children.length > 0) {
              newCollapsed[nodeId] = true;
            }
            state.document!.nodes[nodeId]?.children.forEach(traverse);
          };
          traverse(id);
          return { collapsedNodes: newCollapsed };
        });
        get().autoLayout();
      },
      setFocusNode: (id) => {
        set({ focusNodeId: id });
        get().autoLayout();
      },
      loadDocument: (doc) => {
        set({ document: doc, collapsedNodes: {}, focusNodeId: null });
        get().autoLayout();
      },
      loadTemplate: (template, mode) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (mode === 'replace' || !state.document) {
            return { document: template, collapsedNodes: {}, focusNodeId: null };
          }
          
          // Insert mode: namespace IDs to prevent collisions
          const prefix = `tpl_${Date.now()}_`;
          const newNodes: Record<string, BaseNode> = {};
          const newRows: Record<string, SpreadsheetRow> = {};
          
          Object.values(template.nodes).forEach(node => {
            const newId = `${prefix}${node.id}`;
            const newParentId = node.parentId ? `${prefix}${node.parentId}` : null;
            const newChildren = node.children.map(c => `${prefix}${c}`);
            newNodes[newId] = { ...node, id: newId, parentId: newParentId, children: newChildren };
          });

          if (template.spreadsheetRows) {
            Object.values(template.spreadsheetRows).forEach(row => {
              const newId = `${prefix}${row.row_id}`;
              newRows[newId] = { ...row, row_id: newId };
            });
          }

          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              nodes: { ...state.document.nodes, ...newNodes },
              spreadsheetRows: { ...(state.document.spreadsheetRows || {}), ...newRows },
              templateMetadata: template.templateMetadata, // Keep latest template metadata
            }
          };
        });
        get().autoLayout();
      },
      importSpreadsheet: (rows, mode) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          
          const newRows: Record<string, SpreadsheetRow> = mode === 'replace' ? {} : { ...(state.document.spreadsheetRows || {}) };
          rows.forEach(row => {
            newRows[row.row_id] = row;
          });

          const inputs: DocumentInputs = {
            spreadsheetTruth: newRows,
            hierarchyStructure: state.document.nodes,
            screenshotTemplate: null
          };

          const merged = runHierarchyOrganizer(inputs, false); // false for strictMode to allow creating groups

          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              spreadsheetRows: merged.spreadsheetRows,
              nodes: merged.nodes
            }
          };
        });
        get().autoLayout();
      },
      clearDocument: () => set({ document: null, collapsedNodes: {}, focusNodeId: null }),
      addNode: (parentId, nodeType, label) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          const id = uuidv4();
          const newNode: BaseNode = {
            id,
            key: generateMachineKey(label),
            label,
            nodeType,
            parentId,
            children: [],
          };
          const nodes = { ...state.document.nodes, [id]: newNode };
          if (parentId && nodes[parentId]) {
            nodes[parentId] = {
              ...nodes[parentId],
              children: [...nodes[parentId].children, id],
            };
          }

          let newPos = { x: 0, y: 0 };
          if (parentId && state.document.layout.nodePositions[parentId]) {
            const parentPos = state.document.layout.nodePositions[parentId];
            const siblingsCount = nodes[parentId].children.length;
            newPos = {
              x: parentPos.x + (siblingsCount * 260) - 130,
              y: parentPos.y + 150,
            };
          }

          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              nodes,
              layout: {
                ...state.document.layout,
                nodePositions: {
                  ...state.document.layout.nodePositions,
                  [id]: newPos,
                },
              },
            },
          };
        });
      },
      updateNode: (id, updates) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document || !state.document.nodes[id]) return state;
          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              nodes: {
                ...state.document.nodes,
                [id]: { ...state.document.nodes[id], ...updates },
              },
            },
          };
        });
      },
      deleteNode: (id) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          const nodes = { ...state.document.nodes };
          
          const deleteRecursive = (nodeId: string) => {
            const node = nodes[nodeId];
            if (!node) return;
            node.children.forEach(deleteRecursive);
            delete nodes[nodeId];
          };

          const node = nodes[id];
          if (node && node.parentId && nodes[node.parentId]) {
            nodes[node.parentId] = {
              ...nodes[node.parentId],
              children: nodes[node.parentId].children.filter((childId) => childId !== id),
            };
          }
          deleteRecursive(id);

          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              nodes,
              layout: {
                ...state.document.layout,
                selectedNode: state.document.layout.selectedNode === id ? null : state.document.layout.selectedNode,
              }
            },
          };
        });
      },
      moveNode: (id, newParentId) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          const nodes = { ...state.document.nodes };
          const node = nodes[id];
          if (!node) return state;

          // Remove from old parent
          if (node.parentId && nodes[node.parentId]) {
            nodes[node.parentId] = {
              ...nodes[node.parentId],
              children: nodes[node.parentId].children.filter((childId) => childId !== id),
            };
          }

          // Add to new parent
          if (newParentId && nodes[newParentId]) {
            nodes[newParentId] = {
              ...nodes[newParentId],
              children: [...nodes[newParentId].children, id],
            };
          }

          nodes[id] = { ...node, parentId: newParentId };

          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              nodes,
            },
          };
        });
      },
      selectNode: (id) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              layout: {
                ...state.document.layout,
                selectedNode: id,
              },
            },
          };
        });
      },
      updateNodePosition: (id, position) => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              layout: {
                ...state.document.layout,
                nodePositions: {
                  ...state.document.layout.nodePositions,
                  [id]: position,
                },
              },
            },
          };
        });
      },
      autoLayout: () => {
        set((state) => {
          if (!state.document) return state;
          
          const visibleNodes = getVisibleNodes(state.document.nodes, state.collapsedNodes, state.focusNodeId);
          
          const g = new dagre.graphlib.Graph();
          g.setGraph({ rankdir: 'TB', nodesep: 150, ranksep: 200, marginx: 50, marginy: 50 });
          g.setDefaultEdgeLabel(() => ({}));

          const nodes = state.document.nodes;
          Object.values(nodes).forEach((node) => {
            if (visibleNodes.has(node.id)) {
              g.setNode(node.id, { width: 300, height: 100 });
            }
          });

          Object.values(nodes).forEach((node) => {
            if (visibleNodes.has(node.id)) {
              node.children.forEach((childId) => {
                if (visibleNodes.has(childId)) {
                  g.setEdge(node.id, childId);
                }
              });
            }
          });

          dagre.layout(g);

          const nodePositions: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = { ...state.document.layout.nodePositions };
          g.nodes().forEach((v) => {
            const n = g.node(v);
            nodePositions[v] = { x: n.x - n.width / 2, y: n.y - n.height / 2 };
          });

          return {
            document: {
              ...state.document,
              layout: {
                ...state.document.layout,
                nodePositions,
              },
            },
          };
        });
      },
    }),
    {
      name: 'ui-hierarchy-storage',
    }
  )
);

FILE: src/types/index.ts

export type NodeType = "System" | "Zone" | "Module" | "Container" | "Control";

export const NODE_TYPES: NodeType[] = ["System", "Zone", "Module", "Container", "Control"];

export type MergeState = 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED' | 'DERIVED_CONFIRMED' | 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED' | 'CONFLICT' | 'UNRESOLVED';

export type ProvenanceType = 'TEMPLATE_BASE' | 'USER_EDITED_FROM_TEMPLATE' | 'DECLARED_CONFIRMED' | 'INFERRED_UNCONFIRMED' | 'SPREADSHEET';

export type RowState = 'VALID' | 'VALID_WITH_WARNINGS' | 'INVALID' | 'UNRESOLVED' | 'CONFLICT' | 'RESOLVED';

export interface MergeMetadata {
  source_priority: number;
  source_refs: string[];
  merge_state: MergeState;
  conflict_reason?: string;
  advisory_candidates?: any[];
  user_override_flag?: boolean;
}

export interface BaseNode {
  id: string;
  key: string;
  label: string;
  nodeType: NodeType;
  parentId: string | null;
  children: string[];
  ui?: { x: number; y: number };
  metadata?: {
    confidence?: number;
    type_hint?: string;
    source_reference?: string;
    provenance?: ProvenanceType | string;
    unresolved_fields?: string[];
  };
  mergeMetadata?: Record<string, MergeMetadata>;
}

export interface SpreadsheetRow {
  row_id: string;
  control_name: string;
  function_summary: string;
  subsystem_ref: string;
  control_type: string;
  input_contract: string | null;
  output_contract: string | null;
  grouping_hint: string;
  priority: 'High' | 'Medium' | 'Low' | string;
  
  // Optional
  zone_hint?: string;
  module_hint?: string;
  container_hint?: string;
  related_controls?: string[];
  trigger_type?: string;
  default_state?: string;
  visibility_rule?: string;
  operator_role?: string;
  notes?: string;
  figma_group_hint?: string;
  destination_binding?: string;
  source_binding?: string;
  
  // Metadata
  status: RowState;
  confidence?: number;
  provenance: ProvenanceType | string;
  source_file?: string;
  import_timestamp?: string;
  import_mode?: string;
  source_type?: string;
  row_origin_state?: string;

  // Validation
  validation_errors?: { field: string; type: string; message: string }[];
  
  // Merge
  mergeMetadata?: Record<string, MergeMetadata>;
}

export interface TemplateMetadata {
  template_id: string;
  template_family: string;
  template_variant: 'SIMPLE' | 'EXPANDED';
  template_version: string;
  template_source: 'BUILT_IN_TEMPLATE';
  load_timestamp: string;
  cloned_from_template: boolean;
}

export interface TempTemplate {
  hierarchyTemp: BaseNode[];
  spreadsheetTemp: SpreadsheetRow[];
}

export interface DocumentInputs {
  spreadsheetTruth: Record<string, SpreadsheetRow>;
  hierarchyStructure: Record<string, BaseNode>;
  screenshotTemplate: TempTemplate | null;
}

export interface FigmaControlDetails {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  function_summary?: string;
  subsystem_ref?: string;
  control_type?: string;
  input_contract?: string;
  output_contract?: string;
  grouping_hint?: string;
  priority?: string;
}

export interface FigmaTranslationObject {
  figma_group_id: string;
  figma_group_label: string;
  source_zone_id?: string;
  source_module_id?: string;
  source_container_id?: string;
  source_control_ids: string[];
  spreadsheet_row_refs: string[];
  controls: FigmaControlDetails[];
  interface_role: string;
  grouping_reason: string;
  io_binding_summary: string;
  navigation_binding_summary: string;
  provenance: string;
  unresolved_fields: string[];
  
  // Trace fields
  hierarchy_ref: string;
  spreadsheet_ref: string;
  provenance_type: string;
  export_mode: 'GROUPED_FRAME_EXPORT' | 'CONTROL_SURFACE_EXPORT' | 'WIREFLOW_EXPORT';
  confidence: number;
  unresolved_count: number;
}

export interface ExtractionReport {
  raw_input: string;
  detected_components: {
    label: string;
    type_hint: string;
    confidence: number;
    bounding_box?: any;
    unresolved_fields?: string[];
  }[];
  normalization_issues: string[];
  final_normalized_count: number;
}

export interface GraphLayout {
  nodePositions: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>;
  camera: { x: number; y: number; zoom: number };
  selectedNode: string | null;
  expandedNodes: string[];
}

export interface HierarchyDocument {
  document_id: string;
  document_type: "UIHierarchy";
  schema_version: "1.0";
  title?: string;
  inputs?: DocumentInputs;
  nodes: Record<string, BaseNode>;
  spreadsheetRows?: Record<string, SpreadsheetRow>;
  templateMetadata?: TemplateMetadata;
  extraction_source: "text" | "image" | "manual" | "template";
  original_input_uri?: string;
  metadata: {
    generated: string;
  };
  extraction_report?: ExtractionReport;
  layout: GraphLayout;
}

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig, loadEnv} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(({mode}) => {
  const env = loadEnv(mode, '.', '');
  return {
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
    define: {
      'process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY': JSON.stringify(env.GEMINI_API_KEY),
    },
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modifyâfile watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
    },
  };
});
